The migration section covers how to do migration work: draining the URL_NAME_TO_ACTION registry in test_urls.py to zero (with a recipe for each action type), rolling deploy safety and the two-phase pattern required by independent sentry/getsentry deploys, and the region -&gt; cell rename inclu… ([Harigithub11/sentry](https://github.com/Harigithub11/sentry/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cell-architecture) · ★1)
- **cell-atlas-annotation-agreement-evaluation**: Use when a cell-atlas scRNA-Seq dataset carries multiple conflicting annotation columns (e.g., ICGS2, Seurat3, Monocle3, curated labels) in adata.obs and you need to quantify whether an integrated solution reproduces or refines the reference atlas clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-atlas-annotation-agreement-evaluation))
- **cell-attribute-aware-community-detection**: Use when performing DA-testing of cell-types from scRNA-Seq/snRNA-Seq data where condition labels (age, disease stage, severity, phenotype) exist per cell and standard Louvain, Milo, or DA-seq clustering yields weakly significant composition shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-attribute-aware-community-detection))
- **cell-backlog**: Backlog en Obsidian y su puente con la célula (solo coordinador). Convertir un ítem de Obsidian en GitHub Issue para el líder, procesar los avisos del líder y actualizar el Tablero de control, y armar el reporte de épicas y tareas para Gabriel. Usar en "delegá esto a la célula", "pasale X al líder", "cómo vienen las épicas", "actualizá Obsidian", o al recibir un mensaje del líder. ([Gabriel030/agustina-bermejo-app](https://github.com/Gabriel030/agustina-bermejo-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-backlog))
- **cell-bank-management**: Design cell bank management systems. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with cell-bank-management related tasks. ([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-bank-management) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **cell-barcode-based-cross-modality-label-transfer**: Use when paired multi-omics single-cell data (e.g. SNARE-seq chromatin + expression) share cell barcodes across modalities, and clusters computed on one modality (here the cell-by-peak chromatin side) must be copied to the other for DE/enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-based-cross-modality-label-transfer))
- **cell-barcode-calling-and-filtering**: Use when raw 3' droplet scRNA-seq FASTQ (e.g., GemCode/10x data such as SRA SRP073767) must be turned into a cell-filtered gene-barcode UMI matrix, deciding which barcodes represent cells versus empty droplets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-calling-and-filtering))
- **cell-barcode-demultiplexing**: Use when raw 10x-structured nanopore or PacBio long-read FASTQ reads need cell barcodes found, error-corrected against the 10x v3 whitelist, and assigned per read before alignment/quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-barcode-demultiplexing))
- **cell-barcode-extraction-from-short-reads**: Use when reads are FASTQ/FASTA and a known sequence of interest (barcode, fusion, variant) must be located with tolerated sequencing errors, and reads must be assigned to cell barcodes for single-cell quantification or genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-extraction-from-short-reads))
- **cell-barcode-extraction-scrna-seq**: Use when processing raw 3'-end tag scRNA-seq FASTQ (e.g., MCA GSE108097 or HCL GSE134355) where cell barcodes/UMIs must be extracted from merged inserts and corrected against a whitelist before isoform quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-extraction-scrna-seq))
- **cell-barcode-posterior-error-correction**: Use when raw 10x Genomics droplet scRNA-seq FASTQ reads must have their 16 bp cell barcodes error-corrected (or discarded) prior to UMI filtering and building a gene-barcode count matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-posterior-error-correction))
- **cell-barcode-sharing-hypergeometric-testing**: Use when multiplexed 10x Genomics droplet scRNA-seq samples (e.g. HiSeq 4000-sequenced libraries) must be tested pairwise for more shared cell barcodes than expected by chance, to flag barcode-swapping artefacts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-sharing-hypergeometric-testing))
- **cell-barcode-umi-extraction**: Use when processing HIT-scISOseq CCS reads where primer-based FLNC classification must also yield per-read cellBC and UMI (isoseq_flnc.BarcodeUMI.fastq), or when corrected barcodes/UMIs are needed for isoform count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-barcode-umi-extraction))
- **cell-barcode-umi-extraction-specification**: Use when sequencing reads embed cell barcodes and UMIs at variable-length or non-fixed positions (e.g. 10x Chromium v2/v3, concatenated barcodes) and must be normalized to a simple fixed-position geometry before mapping and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-umi-extraction-specification))
- **cell-barcode-umi-quantification**: Use when you have 10x CellRanger output (filtered_feature_bc_matrix, possorted_genome_bam.bam) and need per-cell pathogen UMI counts for an infected scRNA-seq sample, e.g. HIV EGFP+ cell detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-umi-quantification))
- **cell-barcode-whitelist-correction**: Use when demultiplexing HIT-scISOseq FLNC BarcodeUMI fastq reads into per-cell cDNA reads and observed 10x cell barcodes must be corrected against a whitelist with posterior-probability thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-barcode-whitelist-correction))
- **cell-barcode-whitelist-matching**: Use when single-cell amplicon FASTQ data contain a fixed cell barcode+UMI pattern in R1 (e.g. 16bp barcode + 10bp UMI) and a known cell barcode whitelist is available that extracted barcodes must be matched against. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-barcode-whitelist-matching))

- **cell-based-architecture-designer**: Design cell-based (blast-radius) partitioning for a multi-tenant SaaS — a cell is a self-contained stack subset (compute + data + cache + queue) that serves a subset of tenants end to end, so a failure, bad deploy, or noisy tenant is contained to ONE cell instead of the whole fleet: cell definition, tenant→cell mapping and placement policy, a thin cell-router, cell-by-cell deployment/canary, cross-cell/global concerns, and cell migration/rebalancing. SCALE-STAGE — most SaaS never needs it; recommend cells ONLY when blast-radius reduction is the dominant lever, otherwise say so and stop. - **cell-calling-method-benchmarking**: Use when a raw unfiltered gene-by-barcode count matrix (mtx or h5) from droplet-based scRNA/snRNA-seq needs cell-calling, and multiple callers must be compared against ground-truth labels (HTO singlet/doublet or demuxlet genotypes). - **cell-capacity-report**: Produce a read-only, per-cell capacity report from AMP metrics — DB count, page-cache commit, and disk used against the A12 caps — and recommend add-cell vs rebalance plus flag tenants projected to outgrow a node. - **cell-cell-communication-matrix-factorization**: Use when you have scRNA-seq expression (genes x cells) plus cell labels and need species-agnostic cell-cell communication inference, especially when no curated L-R database exists for the species or condition-specific shared vs unique signals are of interest. - **cell-cell-communication-method-benchmarking**: Use when single-cell RNA-Seq data must be interrogated for ligand-receptor interactions with multiple CCC methods, or when per-method interaction scores need to be combined into a robust consensus ranking via LIANA. - **cell-cell-communication-module-interpretation**: Use when you have pre-computed ligand-receptor CCI predictions (e.g., from CellChat on a scRNA-seq dataset such as GSE72056 melanoma) and need to biologically interpret which interaction groups/signals characterize a specific sender or receiver cell type. - **cell-cell-communication-network-analysis**: Use when you have clustered scRNA-seq cell groups and need to infer significant ligand-receptor communication between them, quantify pathway-level signaling, identify dominant senders/receivers, and group coordinated outgoing/incoming patterns. - **cell-cell-communication-network-inference**: Use when single-cell-resolution spatial transcriptomics data (e.g., seqFISH+ mouse cortex, Seq-Scope mouse colon) with a gene expression matrix plus cell spatial coordinates and cell-type labels is available, and the goal is to estimate which neighboring cell types regulate HVG expression. - **cell-cell-communication-score-computation**: Use when you need to quantify communication potential between an input cell population and partner cell types from transcriptomic data, e.g. comparing outward signaling of CAF-S1 vs CAF-S4 or LPS-stimulated DCs against the Primary Cell Atlas. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cell-communication-score-computation))
- **cell-cell-distance-based-communication-analysis**: Use when you have non-spatial scRNA-seq data plus a limited spatial measurement of a few genes, and need to infer cell-cell communication or the spatial distance/range of a signaling pathway (e.g. Wg ~100 µm, Dpp ~125 µm). - **cell-cell-distance-inference-from-transport-plan**: Use when you have a fitted SpaOTsc transport plan γ* (n cells × m spatial positions) plus a spatial position distance matrix, and you need a pairwise spatial cell-cell distance matrix for downstream signaling or clustering analyses. - **cell-cell-interaction-expression-profiling**: Use when you have developmental series scRNA-seq (e.g., GSE153164 corticogenesis) with inferred L-R pair expression-change values and need to group interactions by temporal activity to pinpoint key developmental time points. - **cell-cell-interaction-label-aggregation**: Use when training or benchmarking a supervised CCI inference model (e.g., DeepCCI's ResNet+GCN interaction model) and per-method significant-interaction calls must be merged into a single consensus label set for classification. - **cell-cell-interaction-network-comparison**: Use when validating AI-inferred single-cell data against a real-profiled ground-truth cohort: you have matched (semi-profiled vs real-profiled) single-cell datasets with shared cell-type annotations and need a quantitative fidelity metric for cell-cell interaction inference. - **cell-cell-interaction-network-inference**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq expression matrix, cell-type labels, and time-point annotations and need to infer which ligand-receptor interactions connect which sender/receiver cell types at a given time point. - **cell-culture-implementation**: Use when applying cell culture implementation. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-implementation) · ★2)
- **cell-culture-tech**: Expert cell culture technician with deep knowledge of aseptic technique, mammalian cell maintenance, passaging protocols, cryopreservation, and contamination troubleshooting. Specializes in HEK293, HeLa, CHO, and primary cells. Use when: cell-culture, aseptic-technique, cell-passaging, tissue-culture, mycoplasma-testing. ([theneoai/awesome-skills](https://github.com/theneoai/awesome-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/persona/research/cell-culture-tech) · ★204 · 3 copies)
- **cell-culture-troubleshooting**: Use when applying cell culture troubleshooting. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-culture-troubleshooting) · ★2)
- **cell-curator**: Annotate pre-clustered AnnData or MuData from local marker, ontology, reference, and multimodal evidence. Use for review-gated cell-type annotation, uncertainty assessment, parent-scoped refinement, and guarded write-back. ([mdmanurung/cell-curator](https://github.com/mdmanurung/cell-curator/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-curator))
- **cell-cycle-age-distribution-modeling**: Use when flow-cytometric single-cell data from an unsynchronized, exponentially growing culture must be placed on real cell-cycle time, and no time-resolved sampling exists to calibrate pseudotime directly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-age-distribution-modeling))
- **cell-cycle-analysis**: Cell cycle phase scoring and visualization for single-cell expression data. Uses Scanpy's score_genes_cell_cycle with Tirosh et al. 2015 S/G2M gene lists on any normalized AnnData (ThreePrimeUntargeted via Loader, scRNA-seq-like, or AnnData from CSV/parquet count matrix). Regressing out cell cycle effects for downstream analysis. Use when the user asks about cell cycle, proliferation, S phase, G2M, or cell state. ([Elembio/agent-skills](https://github.com/Elembio/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/multiomics/skills/cell-cycle-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-effect-removal**: Decision support for removing cell-cycle effects from single-cell RNA-seq expression matrices when an annotated set of cell-cycle control genes is available and downstream analyses require corrected expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-effect-removal--1c3d2484aab0bf12))
- **cell-cycle-effect-removal**: Use when a scRNA-Seq expression matrix (log-scaled, gene-by-gene centered) shows within-cell-type heterogeneity driven by annotated cell-cycle genes that obscures clustering of cell types or substructure, and a known cell-cycle gene set can be retrieved. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-effect-removal--2fb76049d54d8e4e))
- **cell-cycle-effect-removal-method-benchmarking**: Use when scRNA-seq data contain cell-cycle effects confounding subclone or subpopulation delineation, and the goal is to quantify which correction approach (circular vs linear) maximizes subclone separability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-effect-removal-method-benchmarking))
- **cell-cycle-gene-annotation-mapping**: Use when preparing a gene-by-cell expression matrix for ccRemover: gene names must be mapped to human ('human') or mouse ('mouse') cell-cycle annotations to build the if_cc indicator vector before running ccRemover. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-annotation-mapping))
- **cell-cycle-gene-list-curation**: Use when preparing DeepCycle inputs: you need an organism-specific cell-cycle gene list, a base cycling gene (Nusap1 for mESCs, MELK for human fibroblasts), and expression/filter thresholds for gene inclusion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-list-curation))
- **cell-cycle-gene-phase-annotation**: Use when single cells have been reordered along a differentiation trajectory (pseudo-temporal position) and you must cluster gene expression dynamics into temporal waves and check whether cell-cycle gene sets (mitosis vs DNA synthesis) fall into distinct, out-of-phase clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-phase-annotation))
- **cell-cycle-gene-regression-preprocessing**: Use when applying UniTVelo to a pancreas AnnData with spliced/unspliced counts where a known subtle fate transition (e.g., Fev+ to Delta) must be recovered both with and without cell-cycle gene regression, to match a reference fate-mapping result. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-regression-preprocessing))
- **cell-cycle-gene-set-factor-analysis**: Use when labeled (Hoechst or equivalent) scRNA-seq or qPCR single-cell data are available and the goal is to quantify how accurately different cell-cycle characterization methods recover cell-cycle phase labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-gene-set-factor-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-geneset-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when you need to show that periodic genes discovered ab initio from a circular manifold (Cyclum pseudo-time) are more enriched for GO:0007049 cell-cycle terms than genes ranked by principal components on scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-geneset-enrichment-interpretation))
- **cell-cycle-marker-expression-analysis**: Use when a velocity-informed circular trajectory (e.g., Cytopath on the U2OS SMART-Seq2 cell cycle dataset) has been inferred and G1-phase cells must be split into early vs late stages and validated with marker expression tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-marker-expression-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-nicotine-target-nomination**: Use when comparing a treated vs. control single-cell RNA-seq dataset (e.g., 10X Chromium hESC, ~12,280 cells) to find genes whose periodic/circular expression kinetics change between conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-nicotine-target-nomination))
- **cell-cycle-phase-binning**: Use when h5ad data carry a continuous DeepCycle phase annotation (adata.obs['cell_cycle_theta']) with Ms/Mu layers, and you need to quantify how per-cell RNA content (total UMIs) changes around the cell cycle or locate phase transitions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-binning))
- **cell-cycle-phase-classification**: Use when a processed Seurat object or count matrix from single-cell/spatial RNA-seq needs per-cell cell cycle phase assignment (G0, G1, S, G2M), e.g. to quantify quiescent fractions alongside accordion() cell type annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-classification))
- **cell-cycle-phase-classification-svm**: Use when you have single-cell imaging (4i) or single-cell RNA-seq data with a top-k ranked feature subset and ground-truth cell cycle phase labels, and need an accuracy-based comparison of feature selection methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-classification-svm))
- **cell-cycle-phase-correlation-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq data exhibit dominant cell cycling at the onset of differentiation and you need to check whether RNA velocity-based simulation trajectories recapitulate cell cycle stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-correlation-analysis))
- **cell-cycle-phase-inference-deep-learning**: Use when scRNA-seq data contains spliced/unspliced layers, cells are proliferating, and a continuous cell cycle phase assignment is needed from RNA velocity dynamics rather than gene-marker scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-inference-deep-learning))
- **cell-cycle-phase-scoring-tricycle**: Use when you need to evaluate whether forecast or observed single-cell transcriptomic states recapitulate cell-cycle progression, by mapping cells (genes x cells matrices, e.g. scEU-seq hTERT RPE data) to tricycle phase positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-scoring-tricycle))
- **cell-cycle-phase-transition-estimation**: Use when an h5ad annotated by DeepCycle (with obs['cell_cycle_theta'] and scVelo Ms/Mu layers) needs cell-cycle phase labels and per-phase cell fractions, e.g. to validate inferred thetas against flow-cytometry proportions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-phase-transition-estimation))
- **cell-cycle-pseudo-time-inference**: Use when scRNA-seq gene expression is expected to follow a periodic process (e.g., cell cycle) and you need ab initio per-cell pseudo-time or phase ordering without prior marker-gene lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-pseudo-time-inference))
- **cell-cycle-pseudo-time-reconstruction**: Use when given an unsynchronized scRNA-seq expression matrix (log2 TPM+1) and the goal is to recover cell cycle ordering (G1, S, G2/M) or compare pseudo-time methods such as SCUBA, Monocle, TSCAN, Wishbone, or DPT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-pseudo-time-reconstruction))
- **cell-cycle-pseudotime-evaluation**: Use when evaluating an ordering/pseudo-time method for cyclic single-cell data against known stage labels (e.g., G1/S/G2/M), by sweeping cluster number k and comparing correlation-score curves between a proposed method and baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-pseudotime-evaluation))
- **cell-cycle-score-clustering**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq dataset may contain quiescent G0-like cells that confound pseudotime reconstruction, and cell-cycle scores (e.g. reCAT mean scores) are available to identify and remove them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-score-clustering))
- **cell-cycle-scoring-single-cell**: Use when a projected single-cell query contains predicted cycling cells (e.g. Mki67+ exhausted T cells) that need to be quantified and visualized against the projected cell-state labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-scoring-single-cell))
- **cell-cycle-stage-comparison**: Use when you have single-cell Hi-C contact matrices resolved by cell-cycle stage and need to test whether nested TLD hierarchy (modularity, TLD-adjR2, weighted similarity WS) changes across stages such as G1 vs S phase vs G2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-stage-comparison))
- **cell-cycle-stage-interpretation-of-marker-genes**: Use when you need to demonstrate or verify that individual cell cycle marker genes cannot classify single cells into cycle stages, motivating a multi-gene, genome-wide approach such as reCAT. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-stage-interpretation-of-marker-genes))
- **cell-cycle-variation-removal**: Use when single-cell count matrices show cell-cycle-driven confounding (per-cell cell-cycle scores/phases available) that must be removed while preserving biological signal such as subclone or cell-type separability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-cycle-variation-removal))
- **cell-data**: Use to build Cell's data and code deposition plan and the Data and Code Availability statement that lives inside STAR Methods Resource Availability — approved repositories, accessions/DOIs at submission, and Cell's three-bullet availability format with Mendeley Data as Elsevier's default. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-data) · ★1.4k · 5 copies)
- **cell-data**: Draft or audit the data, code, materials and supplemental side of a Cell manuscript: the Resource availability section (Lead contact, Materials availability, Data and code availability), the key resources table with RRIDs, a deposition plan for sequencing, imaging and code, and the supplemental files (Document S1, naming, sizes, Excel tables). Not for data cleaning or statistical analysis. ([yufchen723/JournalFactory](https://github.com/yufchen723/JournalFactory/tree/HEAD/cell-skills/cell-data))
- **cell-data-figure**: 科研数据绘图、顶刊数据图复现与研究数据替换。先按研究问题和数据结构选择可比参考图， 必须用数据编写并实际运行参考复现代码，核验视觉一致性后才能替换用户数据。 最终只交付最终版绘图代码、绘图前数据和 TIF/JPG/PDF 图件。 当用户要求以参考论文图为复现对象、复刻其数据图风格并在门槛通过后替换研究数据时使用； 一般 Python/MATLAB 绘图代码生成、重构或代码规范审核使用 cell-visualization-code。 不用于机制示意图、图形摘要、照片编辑或图片描摹矢量化。 ([yrui-cmd/Cell](https://github.com/yrui-cmd/Cell/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-data-figure) · ★148)
- **cell-density-convex-hull-coverage-quantification**: Use when evaluating scRNA-seq embeddings for trajectory inference suitability and you need a quantitative cell-density metric: cells should be well-distributed (neither over-clumped nor over-dispersed) in the 2D embedding before fitting pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-density-convex-hull-coverage-quantification))
- **cell-design**: Use when generating a design OR when ANY change alters the architecture — a component added, removed, or renamed; an edge or dependency changed; exposure changed; an external/SaaS dependency added or dropped. specs/design/design.cell moves FIRST, before the rest of the design (domain-model.md, flows/, the component design.json files). Covers the grammar, the AEP boundary semantics (where each dependency goes), and the write protocol that drives the live architecture diagram. ([wso2/labs-agentic-engineer](https://github.com/wso2/labs-agentic-engineer/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-design) · ★25)
- **cell-design**: Del ticket al mockup aprobado (solo líder técnico). Fase de diseño de los tickets tipo B (cambia una pantalla) y C (pantalla nueva): mockup con componentes reales, ux-advisor, Artifact y OK de Gabriel. Usar cuando el plan marque tipo B o C, o "cell-design", "armá el mockup". ([Gabriel030/agustina-bermejo-app](https://github.com/Gabriel030/agustina-bermejo-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-design))
- **cell-detection**: Cell segmentation in fluorescence microscopy images. Supports Cellpose/cpsam (Cellpose 4.0) with additional backends planned. Produces segmentation masks, per-cell morphology metrics (area, diameter, centroid, eccentricity), overlay figures, and a report.md. ([ClawBio/ClawBio](https://github.com/ClawBio/ClawBio/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-detection) · ★1.2k · 9 copies)
- **cell-detection**: Comprehensive guide to cell detection. Master the concepts, implementation, best practices, and real-world applications of cell detection in professional environments. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-ml/cell-detection) · ★1)
- **cell-diagram**: Use when asked to draw, write, or edit a cell architecture diagram or a `.cell` file, when working with Cell Architect or @kanushka/cell-diagram-react, or when a system needs to be shown as a cell with what it exposes and what it depends on. ([kanushka/cell-architect](https://github.com/kanushka/cell-architect/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-diagram) · ★5)
- **cell-dimension-value-extraction**: Use when processing natural-language prokaryotic taxonomic descriptions to populate a taxon-by-character matrix with cell dimension (diameter, width, length) measurement states and units. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cell-dimension-value-extraction))
- **cell-divide**: Reproductive function. Copies the root .Codex/skills/ genome into a target section's .Codex/skills/ location (or deeper), appends the parent commit SHA to each child SKILL.md lineage, and registers the new cell in lattice/knowledge/skills_registry. INERT in Phase 1 — do not execute recursive division yet. ([JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform](https://github.com/JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cell-divide) · ★3)
- **cell-divide**: Reproductive function. Copies the root .claude/skills/ genome into a target section's .claude/skills/ location (or deeper), appends the parent commit SHA to each child SKILL.md lineage, and registers the new cell in lattice/knowledge/skills_registry. INERT in Phase 1 — do not execute recursive division yet. ([JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform](https://github.com/JeromyJSmith/lattice-platform/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-divide) · ★3)
- **cell-dynamics**: Understand cellular differentiation, state transitions, and dynamic processes through trajectory and velocity analysis. Use when user wants to study development, differentiation, cell fate decisions, or temporal dynamics. Triggers: "trajectory", "pseudotime", "RNA velocity", "differentiation", "cell fate", "how do cells develop", "lineage", "dynamics", "scVelo", "CellRank". ([cafferychen777/ChatSpatial](https://github.com/cafferychen777/ChatSpatial/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cell-dynamics))
- **cell-embedding-reliability-classification**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq/CyTOF object has been embedded with t-SNE or UMAP and you need to quantify which cell embeddings are dubious vs trustworthy, or to pick hyperparameters (perplexity, min.dist, n.neighbors) by minimizing dubious embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-embedding-reliability-classification))
- **cell-explore**: Explore Virtuoso cellviews - list libraries, cells, instances, nets, layers, and hierarchy. Use when browsing a design, understanding circuit topology, or inspecting layout/schematic contents. ([deanyou/virtuoso-cli](https://github.com/deanyou/virtuoso-cli/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cell-explore) · ★45 · 4 copies)
- **cell-fate-branch-allocation-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq cells from perturbed (e.g. knockout GMP) populations must be allocated to branches of an already-inferred wild-type branching trajectory to measure fate bias between, e.g., monocyte and granulocyte branches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-branch-allocation-quantification))
- **cell-fate-dynamical-analysis**: Use when a validated biomolecular network model must be simulated via dynamical analysis to obtain cell fate outcomes (normal proliferation, apoptosis), or when its robustness to small input perturbations must be quantified by SEM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-fate-dynamical-analysis))
- **cell-fate-dynamical-manifold-construction**: Use when you have a cell-cell rwTPM and DynamicalAnalysis output (attractor basins, coarse-grained P_ij, soft membership rho*(x)) from MuTrans and need to visualize the manifold, place attractor basins and saddle/transition cells on it, or sanity-check basin structure before lineage (MPPT) or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-dynamical-manifold-construction))
- **cell-fate-fraction-quantification**: Use when you have simulated single-cell trajectories from a PRESCIENT perturbation run (e.g., TF overexpression/knockdown sweeps) and need to measure how perturbation magnitude shifts fate fractions at the terminal time point, with statistical testing of reciprocal fate changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-fraction-quantification))
- **cell-fate-lineage-classification**: Use when you have GEMLI-predicted lineage assignments plus cell type annotations (e.g., DCIS vs inv_tumor cells from GSE148093 breast cancer biopsies) and need to separate symmetric from asymmetric (mixed-fate) lineages and profile DEGs per fate class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-lineage-classification))
- **cell-fate-probability-inference**: Use when a scRNA-seq task asks to transfer continuous (non-discrete) annotations such as 7-dimensional cell fate probability vectors between two sequencing runs of the Tusi hematopoietic progenitor dataset, and to rank annotation-transfer methods by JSD. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-inference))
- **cell-fate-probability-normalization**: Use when RNA velocity (scvelo spliced/unspliced AnnData) plus Cytopath consensus trajectory steps are available and you need per-cell alignment scores, trajectory assignments, pseudotime, fate probabilities, or differentiation potential per cell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-normalization))
- **cell-fate-probability-quantification**: Use when scRNA-seq data with cluster annotations and an RNA velocity field are available and you need per-cell probabilities of reaching defined terminal fates (e.g., alpha, beta, epsilon) plus aggregate end-fate frequencies from stochastic Markov sampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-quantification))
- **cell-fate-probability-validation-pancreatic-endocrine**: Use when simulating cell-fate decisions from a preprocessed pancreatic endocrine AnnData with cluster annotations and differentiation potentials, and RNA velocity is unreliable, so trajectories must be biased by the differentiation-potential gradient toward terminal fates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-probability-validation-pancreatic-endocrine))
- **cell-fate-propensity-quantification**: Use when a validated biomolecular regulatory network (e.g., the 197-node/744-edge Cho et al. network) has been simulated with CanSeer's DA and stable-state probabilities must be converted into per-fate propensity values for validation or treatment comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-fate-propensity-quantification))
- **cell-fate-terminal-state-detection-evaluation**: Use when a single-cell trajectory dataset may harbor elusive or low-population terminal fates (e.g., multifurcating, cyclic, or disconnected topologies) and you need automated terminal-state calling plus a quantitative fate-recovery score against known labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-terminal-state-detection-evaluation))
- **cell-fate-transition-scoring**: Use when evaluating RNA velocity or latent-trajectory outputs on a multi-lineage, multi-batch scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., mouse gastrulation AnnData with spliced/unspliced layers) and you need quantitative transition, batch-mixing, and velocity-consistency scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-fate-transition-scoring))
- **cell-favorability-score-computation**: Use when a significant directed cell-cell interaction network (e.g., cytotoxic cells → tumor cells) has already been inferred from bulk expression plus survival data with TimiGP and you need per-cell favorable/unfavorable prognostic rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-favorability-score-computation))
- **cell-figure**: Plan, draw, revise, audit and export figures for a Cell (Cell Press) manuscript in Python or R: main figures, Figure S, and the graphical abstract. Sizes, fonts, lines, colour and file rules are Cell's own (85 / 114 / 174 mm, Arial, about 6-8 pt, capital panel letters, TIFF or PDF). Not for interactive dashboards or statistics-only analysis. ([yufchen723/JournalFactory](https://github.com/yufchen723/JournalFactory/tree/HEAD/cell-skills/cell-figure))
- **cell-figure-guide**: Cell (Cell Press) figure preparation: resolution (300-1000 DPI), formats (TIFF/PDF), RGB color, Avenir/Arial fonts, uppercase panel labels, strict image manipulation policies. - **cell-specific-network-construction**: Use when a normalized scRNA-seq gene expression matrix (m genes × n cells) needs to be transformed into direct gene–gene association networks per cell, or into a CNDM for downstream clustering/dimension reduction. - **cell-state-annotation-nearest-neighbor**: Use when query scRNA-seq count matrices (e.g. 10x/hdf5 CD45+ TIL or P14 T cell data) must be assigned reference cell-state labels and subtype frequencies by projecting them onto a curated TIL or viral-infection reference atlas. - **cell-state-composition-frequency-analysis**: Use when projecting scRNA-seq query data (e.g., Regnase-1-null vs WT CD8+ TILs) onto a reference atlas and you need cell-state frequency compositions per condition to detect enrichment of subtypes like Tpex or Tex. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-state-composition-frequency-analysis))
- **cell-state-gene-signature-overlap-analysis**: Use when validating a learned cell-state classifier (e.g., Pollock explain-mode DWGs) against curated marker databases like PanglaoDB, or when asked which cell states' feature scores recover known markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-state-gene-signature-overlap-analysis))
- **cell-state-transition-network-modeling**: Use when analyzing TSCS single-cell RT-PCR or RNA-seq data from two consecutive stages of a cell-state transition (e.g., ICM-&gt;EPI, ICM-&gt;PE) and needing a directed, weighted, sparse GRN without constructing pseudo-time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-state-transition-network-modeling))
- **cell-statistics**: Audit or draft the statistical reporting of a Cell manuscript: replicate unit, biological vs technical replicates, tests, multiple testing, figure-legend statistics, and the STAR Methods section 'Quantification and statistical analysis'. Use for legend statistics checks, pseudoreplication in single-cell or spatial data, and reviewer concerns about statistics. Computes new analyses only when the user supplies data and asks. ([yufchen723/JournalFactory](https://github.com/yufchen723/JournalFactory/tree/HEAD/cell-skills/cell-statistics))
- **cell-stem-cell**: Use when targeting Cell Stem Cell or deciding whether a stem-cell or regenerative-biology manuscript fits this Cell Press venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/cell-stem-cell) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cell-submission**: Use as the final preflight before submitting to Cell — a complete checklist across significance, narrative, Highlights/eTOC/Graphical Abstract, Summary, figures, STAR Methods, data, references, and required files. Bundles the checklist and cover-letter templates. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Cell-Skills/skills/cell-submission) · ★1.4k · 5 copies)
- **cell-submission**: 将已经写好的 SCI 论文整理为目标期刊投稿包。用于正文已完成后的期刊格式适配、参考文献编排、图表与补充材料整理、cover letter、title page、作者声明、报告清单和投稿系统字段准备，以及转投格式转换。不用于从零写论文、重做研究、编造缺失方法或默认代为正式投稿。可承接 sci-manuscript-writer 的 Manuscript.docx。 已有英文正文的深度语言润色、论证重组与证据强度编辑使用 cell-manuscript-editing。 ([yrui-cmd/Cell](https://github.com/yrui-cmd/Cell/tree/HEAD/skills/cell-submission) · ★148)
- **cell-subpopulation-identification-in-2d-embedding**: Use when scRNA-seq data contain known or suspected heterogeneous cell populations (e.g., developmental stages E3–E7 or lineage compartments like ICM/PE/EPI vs TE) that should be recovered as separable clusters in a low-dimensional 2D visualization. - **cell-subsampling-and-label-shuffling**: Use when you need to test how sensitive CCC inference methods (CellChat, CellPhoneDB, Connectome, NATMI, SingleCellSignalR, iTALK, Cytotalk via LIANA) are to cell-level noise—reduced cell numbers or erroneous cell-type annotation—in a scRNA-seq dataset. - **cell-subset-filtering-by-reference-annotation**: Use when a heterogeneous PBMC scRNA-seq object has coarse reference-based (SingleR) lineage labels — e.g. a T/NK subset — and the goal is to recluster that subset to expose rare subtypes or doublets hidden by the annotation. - **cell-subset-resampling-across-patients**: Use when you need to characterize computational scaling (wall-clock time, peak memory) of MaCroDNA, clonealign, or Seurat on patient-stratified scDNA/scRNA data by re-running at controlled sampling rates such as 0.5–1.0. - **cell-subtype-fine-annotation-with-template-classifiers**: Use when a normalized single-cell tumor scRNA-seq expression matrix needs cell subtype labels beyond the five major TME types, using pretrained subtype templates rather than reference-free clustering. - **cell-subtype-supervised-classification**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq object from tumor tissue needs fine-grained cell subtype labels within the five TME compartments (T.cells, Myeloid.cells, B.cells, Fibroblast, Endothelial), or when cross-dataset subtype label similarity must be quantified. - **cell-type-annotation**: scRNA-seq cell type annotation - read before any annotation skills. Covers package availability, tissue- and state-matched references, marker panels, confidence and marker validation of transferred labels, doublets, unresolved clusters. - **cell-type-annotation-mapping**: Use when a single-cell expression matrix (genes x cells) must be paired with a cell-annotation metafile before running NATMI ExtractEdges.py, or when cell-type labels need to be validated so edges are attributed to the right cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-mapping--f89feabe09b6762f))
- **cell-type-annotation-marker-genes**: Use when you have a cell-by-factor or low-dimensional embedding from paired multi-omics data (e.g., Mowgli on TEA-seq PBMC) and need per-cell type labels grounded in canonical gene/protein markers plus an independent reference annotation. - **cell-type-annotation-model-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a cell type annotation tool's accuracy on reference-to-query label transfer, or when assessing whether annotation performance depends on expert-knowledge masks vs. parameter-matched random masks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-model-evaluation))
- **cell-type-annotation-model-training**: Use when confident cell type labels from a consensus of annotation tools must be spread to unlabeled cells in scRNA-seq data via a graph convolutional network, or when tuning GCN hyperparameters per dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-model-training--b47d8a124ee0b25f))
- **cell-type-annotation-model-training**: Use when benchmarking how subsampling/sketching (e.g. scValue sketches at 0.02–0.10 fractions) affects supervised cell-type annotation accuracy on .h5ad scRNA-seq datasets with known ground-truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-model-training--b25b15dbb1c13775))
- **cell-type-annotation-pipeline-comparison-benchmarking**: Use when you must quantify resource/accuracy trade-offs of cell type annotation pipelines on identical feature subsets of an scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., reproducing a Table-3-style comparison of accuracy, memory, and execution time). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-pipeline-comparison-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-annotation-plotting**: Use when comparing imputed vs. unimputed scRNA-seq matrices (e.g., Jurkat-293T or Zeisel datasets) to assess whether imputation improves clustering of annotated cell-type subpopulations, as in Fig. 5 of the AutoImpute paper. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-plotting))
- **cell-type-annotation-scoring-against-reference-database**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters (e.g., from Seurat) need automatic, knowledge-free cell type annotation by scoring cluster marker genes against a tissue-specific cell taxonomy reference database rather than manual marker inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-scoring-against-reference-database))
- **cell-type-annotation-standardization**: Use when combining scRNA-seq studies whose free-text cell annotations differ, e.g. before rendering a cross-study comparison of expression across aligned cell types in Cellenium (H5AD-imported studies with ontologized metadata). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-standardization))
- **cell-type-annotation-transfer-with-mlp-classifier**: Use when semi-profiled single-cell data (e.g. scSemiProfiler output for a COVID-19 cohort with Healthy/Asymptomatic/Mild/Moderate/Severe/Critical conditions) is unlabeled, and annotated representative single-cell reference data exists to train a classifier for label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-annotation-transfer-with-mlp-classifier))
- **cell-type-annotation-visualization**: Reference for annotating scywalker long-read single-nuclei per-cell gene counts with cell types from a markerset and visualizing the assignments on UMAP in the QC report. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cell-type-annotation-visualization))
- **cell-type-balanced-subset-allocation**: Use when a large scRNA-seq AnnData (e.g. a 428k-cell gut atlas in .h5ad) must be subsampled to a fixed sketch size while preserving cell-type diversity and rare populations for downstream annotation/ML tasks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-balanced-subset-allocation))
- **cell-type-biomarker-ranking-extraction**: Use when a trained scRNA-seq cell-type classifier (e.g. scaLR DNN on anndata .h5ad data) must be converted into per-class ranked gene lists, or when top-100 biomarkers must be checked for recall against a curated reference like CellMarker2.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-biomarker-ranking-extraction))
- **cell-type-bootstrap-association-testing**: Use when GWAS summary statistics and an annotated single-cell Seurat object are available and the goal is to rank predefined cell types for trait/disease association at FDR &lt; 0.05 (e.g., AD vs. OPCs/microglia, severe COVID-19 vs. PBMC subsets). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-bootstrap-association-testing))
- **cell-type-classification-accuracy-assessment**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters have been produced and reference cell-type labels exist, and you need ARI plus per-cell-type classification accuracy (e.g., alpha/beta cells) to quantify clustering fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-accuracy-assessment))
- **cell-type-classification-accuracy-computation**: Use when benchmarking supervised cell-type classifiers on annotated scRNA-seq data and you need repeatable accuracy and balanced-accuracy estimates for SciBet, scmap, and Seurat v3 across many datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-accuracy-computation))
- **cell-type-classification-evaluation**: Use when a labeled single-cell RNA-seq AnnData (cell x gene, 'class_label' column) needs automated cell-type label transfer with measurable accuracy/F1, including imbalanced, many-class (e.g., 42-type mouse brain) or small-training-set settings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-evaluation--942de924bfb6c47e))
- **cell-type-classification-evaluation**: Use when you have a set of k selected marker genes on a labeled scRNA-seq AnnData object and need to quantify how well those markers discriminate cell types via classifier performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-evaluation--b06477fbcf578dba))
- **cell-type-classification-macro-f1-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell type annotation models on a held-out test set and you need donor-generalizing performance metrics such as macro F1-score and cross-entropy loss. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-macro-f1-evaluation))
- **cell-type-classification-performance-evaluation**: Use when you have trained cell-type CRE classifiers (e.g., BOM/XGBoost on motif counts) and need to quantify prediction accuracy, such as fraction of elements assigned to the correct cell type or cross-species transfer rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-performance-evaluation))
- **cell-type-classification-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing or benchmarking the running time of automatic scRNA-seq cell-type identification methods on subsets of varying training/testing sizes, e.g. reproducing the Fig. 4 speed ranking on the mouse brain dataset GSE116470. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-classification-runtime-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-clustering-ari-benchmarking**: Use when reproducing the LUAD/HCC827-H1975-H2228 scRNA-seq clustering benchmark across three platforms with the 2267 common HVGs, or when comparing ARI of unsupervised batch-correction clustering to published values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-clustering-ari-benchmarking))
- **cell-type-clustering-assessment**: Use when an scRNA-Seq matrix has been corrected for the cell-cycle effect and you must verify that cell-type signal was retained while cell-cycle heterogeneity was removed, comparing original vs corrected matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-clustering-assessment))
- **cell-type-clustering-unknown-subtypes**: Use when raw scRNA-seq read count matrices from multiple batches contain cell types that are unknown a priori, and clustering plus batch-effect correction must be performed jointly without preliminary normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-clustering-unknown-subtypes))
- **cell-type-colored-embedding-visualization**: Use when a 2D ivis embedding of scRNA-seq data has been computed and you need to visually validate cell-type segregation and global cluster relationships against published figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-colored-embedding-visualization))
- **cell-type-comparative-epigenomics**: Use when CpG-level methylation calls (e.g., Roadmap WGBS) must be summarized per interval set and compared across cell types to test differential methylation level/variation between HOT regions and control regions like non-HOT CpG islands. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-comparative-epigenomics))
- **cell-type-composition-input-construction**: Use when preparing seq-based ST data (e.g., 10X Visium slices such as the 12 human DLPFC slices) for Splane spatial domain identification, which requires per-spot cell type composition rather than gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-composition-input-construction))
- **cell-type-composition-quantification-across-conditions**: Use when comparing cell-state or cell-type proportions between conditions (e.g., KO vs WT) in scRNA-seq data where cells have been projected onto a reference atlas and assigned discrete cell-state labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-composition-quantification-across-conditions))
- **cell-type-composition-regression-analysis**: Use when a single-cell/nucleus atlas has cell-type or cell-state annotations plus per-donor covariates, and the goal is to test whether cell-type fractions differ across a condition (e.g. obesity, depot) while controlling for covariates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-composition-regression-analysis))
- **cell-type-consistency-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell type annotation accuracy of cluster-based vs cell-based methods on scRNA-seq datasets with known reference labels, or when quantifying how swapping the underlying reference database affects annotation consistency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-consistency-evaluation))
- **cell-type-consistency-rate-computation**: Use when evaluating how accurately an automatic cell-type annotation method labels clusters of scRNA-seq data against manually curated cell types, and a comparable consistent-rate table across multiple methods is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-consistency-rate-computation))
- **cell-type-correlation-strategy-selection**: Use when training a supervised single-cell label-assignment model on scRNA-seq data with known cell types and you must decide between a flat vs hierarchical classifier based on how highly correlated the training cell types are. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-correlation-strategy-selection))
- **cell-type-correspondence-mapping**: Use when visualizing two or more cellular-resolution SRT sections jointly (e.g., Stereo-seq axolotl DPI-10 vs DPI-15) and cell-type labels differ across sections, so naive default palettes misalign colors between biologically related or confusable cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-correspondence-mapping))
- **cell-type-count-aggregation**: Use when you have a cell-by-feature scRNA-seq or scATAC-seq count matrix with per-cell type labels and need across-cell-type expression/accessibility profiles for Spearman/k-medoids clustering or Kendall's Tb correlation validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-count-aggregation))
- **cell-type-count-matrix-aggregation**: Use when a single-cell count matrix (e.g., scRNA-seq from MOSim sc_mosim) must be reduced to per-cell-type average profiles before clustering, PCA, or cross-omics correlation validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cell-type-count-matrix-aggregation))
- **cell-type-deconvolution-method-evaluation**: Use when comparing cell type identification or gene expression decomposition methods on simulated single-slice spatial transcriptomics benchmarks with paired scRNA-seq references and ground-truth per-cell assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-deconvolution-method-evaluation))
- **cell-type-deconvolution-wnnls**: Use when estimating cell type proportions from bulk RNA-seq read counts with a multi-subject single-cell reference, especially for closely related cell types and without pre-selected marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cell-type-deconvolution-wnnls))
